3a1(rgyud): @#//ba|so sor rab tu gnas pa dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs

3b1(rgyud): du byung ba/ting nge 'dzin bye brag dang /rdzu 'phrul dang /rnam par thar pa'i dkyil 'khor byang chub

4a2(rgyud): te/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi spyod pa ting nge 'dzin gyi khyad par dang /sa chen po thob par bya ba dang /
4a3(rgyud): rdo rje'i gdan gnon pa dang /bdud 'dul ba dang /chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /nyan thos dang /rang 
4a4(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi nges par 'byung ba dang /lha rnams dang /mi rnams su bskyed par bya ba dang /
4a5(rgyud): sdug bsngal dang /dbul ba dang /nad thams cad zhi bar byed pa/longs spyod kyis phyug pa/'dren par 
4b1(rgyud): byed pa/'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i sngags kyi spyod pas 'phangs par byed mi byed pa/re ba thams cad yongasu rdzogs 
4b2(rgyud): par byed pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis gsung 'bras bu med par byed pa ma yin pa/bcom ldan 'das kyis byams pa'i 
4b3(rgyud): thugs dang /phan pa'i thugs dang /bdag dang sems can thams cad la thugs brtse ba nye bar bzung nas de bshad du gsol/
4b4(rgyud): de nas thub pas thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor gnas gtsang ma'i gnas la sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis rnam 
4b5(rgyud): par gzigs nas dag pa'i yul la rnam par spros te/yul rnam par 'jig pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs so//

5a1(rgyud): @#/ O /par bskul ba zhes bya ba'i 'od pung ste/'od bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du ma'i 'khor gyis bcom ldan 'das 
5a2(rgyud): la lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/steng gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams su seng nas byang shar gyis phyogasu brgya stong gi gang 
5a3(rgyud): ga'i klung gi 'jig rten gyis khams 'das nas/mo tog dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rjes su zhugs te/gang na me tog 
5a4(rgyud): kun tu skyes pa'i rgyal po'i dbang po zhes bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa bzhugs paro//der yang 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa sngon gyi smon 
5a5(rgyud): lam gyis dbang gis/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i spyod pa dang ldan pa rnams dang lhan cig gnas par gyur to//'jam
5a6(rgyud): dpal gzhon nur gyur pas 'od zer gyi snang ba de mthong nas/bzhin cung zad 'dzum pa dang ldan par gyur nas byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs 
5b1(rgyud): de dag la smras pa/rgyal ba'i sras dag 'od 'di ni nga la yang dag par bskal ba'i phyir 'ongs pa yin gyis/khyed cag g.yeng ba med par gyis ces smras so//
5b2(rgyud): de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'jam dpal gzhan du gyur pa mig mi 'dzams zhing kha bye bas/'od gang na ba de logasu mngon du bltas 
5b3(rgyud): nas 'dug go/de nas bskul ba'i 'od des me tog dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i 'jig rten gyis khams snang ba chen pos snang bar byas te/me tog kun du 
5b4(rgyud): skyes pa zhes bya ba'i rgyal po'i dbang po de bzhin gshegs pa la lan gsum bskar ba byas te byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po 'jam dpa' gzhon 
5b5(rgyud): nur gyur pa'i spyi bor nub par gyur to//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa bstan las langs te/bcom ldan 'das me tog kun du yang dag par skyes pa'i 
5b6(rgyud): rgyal po'i dbang po de bzhinagashegs pa la lan gsum skor ba byas te/spyi bos phyag 'tshal nas pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/
5b7(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das me tog kun du skyes pa'i rgyal po'i dbang po la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa zhes bya ba/de bzhin gshegs 
6a1(rgyud): @#//pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis bdag la dgongs te/bcom ldan 'das bdag ni bcom ldan 'das 
6a2(rgyud): shAkya thub pa la blta ba dang /phyag bya ba dang /bsnyan bkur bya ba dang /sngags thams cad kyis spyed pa dang /dkyil 'khor bsgrub thabs dang /dkyil 'khor 
6a3(rgyud): dang /cho ga dang /gsang ba dang /cho ga zhib se dang /cho ga'i ngo bo dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs dang /gsang ba dang /phyag rgya dang /
6a4(rgyud): dbang bskur ba bstan pa dang /sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khamasu 'di nas 
6a5(rgyud): 'chi bar 'tshal lo//de skad ces bcom ldan 'das me tog kan te skyes pa'i rgyal po'i dbang po re bzhin gshegs pa la gsol pa dang /'jam dpal gzhon 
6a6(rgyud): nur gyur pa ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/phyogs bzhi mtha' yas mu med pa'i steng dang /'og dang /thad ka thams cad dang ldan pa'i phyogs 
6a7(rgyud): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis yang dag par gang bar gyur te//'jig rten gyi khams de'i bcom ldan 'das dang bcas pas rgyal ba'i sras po 
6b1(rgyud): legs so legs so//'di lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin gyi khyad par la snyems par bzhugs pa ni/nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis kyang mi nus/byang chub 
6b2(rgyud): sems dpa' dang /spyod pa la zhugs pa dang /sa bcu la gnas pa dag gis kyang snyems par zhugs par mi nus so//de nas bcom ldan 'das me tog kun tu yang 
6b3(rgyud): dag par skyes pa'i rgyal po de bzhin gshegs pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de rnams dang lhan gcig tu yang dag par 'grogs te/'jam dpal gzhon nur 
6b4(rgyud): gyur pa 'di'i snying po mchog tu bsang ba/de na thams cad sgrub pa'i snying po gsungs pa ni/yi ge gcig pa zhes bya ba'i mchog tu gsang ba/sems can thams 
6b5(rgyud): cad kyi las thams cad byed pa/sngags kyi spyod pa khyad par can bzang po yang sgrub par byed pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'da'as me tog yang dag par skyes pa'i rgyal pa'i 
6b6(rgyud): dbang po//de bzhin gshegs pa yud tsam zhig cang mi gsang bar gyur te/thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'jig rten thams cad la sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis gzigs 
6b7(rgyud): te sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' de dag la yang dag par dgongs nas/byams pa'i thugs kyis sngags gsungs so//na ma sarba bud+d+ha nam/oM maM////
6b8(rgyud): 'di ni 'jam dpal gyi snying po mchog yin te/las thams cad byed pa'o//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa ting nge 'dzin de las langs nas/dper na skyes bu 
7a1(rgyud): @#//stobs dang ldan pa lag pa skum pa las brkyang ba 'am/se gol togs pa tsam 'am/mig 'byed 'dzums tsam la rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs 
7a2(rgyud): kyi shugs blo ting nge 'dzin ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis khyad par 'phrul pa zhes bya ba la snyoms par zhugs te/mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams su chas te song 
7a3(rgyud): ba dang /steng gi nam mkha'i dkyil na nor bu rin po che rab tu gnas pa'i gnas gtsang ma'i rigs kyi lha rnams su chas te/gnas gtsang ma'i gnas de thams cad 'od chen po'i 
7a4(rgyud): snang bas gsal bar byas te/'od rin po ches rab tu brgyan cing gsal ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so/ O /rin po che rnam pa tha dad pa du ma'i khang 
7a5(rgyud): pa brtsegs pa dang /rin po che'i gdugs dang /dpag tshad brgya stong khrag du ma'i rgya che ba'i gos dang /dar mang po'i tshogs kyis nye bar mdzes par byas pa'i 'dzaM 
7a6(rgyud): dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la 'di skad ces bka' bstsal te/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa de'i dus la bab par shes na song shig/'on kyang nga'i bkas bcom 
7a7(rgyud): ldan 'das shAkya thub pa la gnod pa nyung ngam/nyam nga ba nyung ngam/skyed pa yang ngam/bde ba la reg par gnas sam zhes zhus par bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 
7a8(rgyud): 'das me tog yang dag par skyes pa rgyal po'i dbang po de bzhin gshegs pas/'jam dpal gzhon nuragyur pa la 'di skad ces bga' stsal to//gzhan yang 
7b1(rgyud): gzhon nu gang ga'i klung gi brgya stong gi bye ma snyed kyi de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis khyod kyi sngags kyi spyod pa dang /
7b2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor dang /cho ga zhib mo dang /gsang ba dang /dbang bskur ba dang /phyag rgya'i tshogs dang /cho ga dang /sbyin bsreg dang /bzlas pa dang /
7b3(rgyud): brtul zhugs bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa/sems can thams cad dga' bar byed pa/'od rin po che rab 'byams 'das pa dang /da ltar byung 
7b4(rgyud): ba dang /ma 'ongs par shes pa/rgyal po'i dbang phyug lung ston par byed pa/sngags zla ba yul gyi mthar 'gro bar byed pa/mi snang bar byed pa dang /dus   
7b5(rgyud): dang dam tshig rab 'byams kyi tshogs thams cad dang /'jig rten pa ma lus pa dang /'jig rten las 'das pa'i sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dang /
7b6(rgyud): 'phags pa nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi nges par 'byung ba'i spyod pa thar thug pa gsungs pa dang /gsung ba dang /gsung bar 'gyur ba yin te/de ltar rjesu 
7b7(rgyud): yi rang ba nyid de/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa de'i dus la bab par shes nas song shig/shAkya thub pa'i spyan sngar mngon sum du chos kyi rnam grangs 'di 
7b8(rgyud): thos par 'gyur ro//khyod kyang ston par 'gyur ro//'dir yang gsungs pa na//na maHsarba ta thA ga ta naM/a tsin+t+ya a pra ti ta sha sa naM/oM sarba ra a pra ti 
8a1(rgyud): @#//ha ta sha sa na/ku mA ra rU pa ha ri na/oM hUM hUM phaT phaT swAhA//'di ni 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa'i rtsa ba'i sngags so//de bzhin 
8a2(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying pos te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis gsungs pa la/gsung bar 'gyur la/khyod kyang mi mjed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams su song nas/
8a3(rgyud): dbye ba dang /rnam par dbye ba brgya cher sten par 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa zhes bya bas kyang las thams cad byed par rjes su gnang bar 'gyur ro//

8a6(rgyud): pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa la mchod pa bya ba'i phyir/lha'i me tog rnam par bkod pa dang /
8a7(rgyud): rgyal mtshan dang /ba dan dang /phreng ba'i tshogs dang /rin po che'i dril bu'i dra bas 'brel ba snyan pa'i sgra sgrogs pa'i phyir mi ldog pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i 
8b1(rgyud): sa thob par byed pa/lha'i dri dang /byug pa dang /phreng ba dang /phye ma'i char 'bab par mngon par sprul to//de nas gnas gtsang ma'i rigs kyi   lha'i bu lha'i bu 
8b2(rgyud): rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba'i cho 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul pa mthang nas/ba spu ldang zhing gnas g.yo ba mthong nas snying 'dar 
8b3(rgyud): zhing dngang ste/ci bdag cag gi 'byor pa nyam par ma gyur tam/zhes pan tsun du rings par 'gro zhing skad mthon po'i smra ba rtsom par byed de/'di skad du bcom 
8b4(rgyud): ldan 'das yongs su skyab tu gsol/shAkya thub pa yongs su skyab tu (zhal)gsol to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis thams cad dang ldan pa'i gnas gtsang ma'i

8b6(rgyud): nur gyur pa 'di ni me tog yang dag par skyes pa'i rgyal po de bzhin gshegs pa de'i spyan snga nas nga la blta ba dang /phyag bya ba dang /bsnyen bkur ba dang /
8b7(rgyud): don chen po'i spyod pa dang /sngags kyi tshig dang /rgya che zhing rmad du byung ba'i chos kyi tshig ston pa'i phyir 'ongs pa yin no//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur

9b1(rgyud): la khyod phyag 'tshal//chos kun rnam par rtogs mdzad pa//chos thams cad kyi de bzhin nyid spros pa med pa'i ngo bo yang dag par rjes su zhugs zhing ston par
9b2(rgyud): byed pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//shes pa dang /shes bya'i dngos po thams cad dang /'dus byas dang /'dus ma byas dang /
9b3(rgyud): theg pa gsum gyi lam dang /mya ngan las 'das pa'i lam la 'jog par byed pa dang gnas par byed pa thams cad mkhyen pa khyod la phyag 'tshal lo //

9b7(rgyud): po de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig pa dang zhabasu ldan pa/bde bar gshegs pa/'jig rten mkhyen pa/skyes
10a1(rgyud): @#//bu 'dul ba/ba lo bsgyur ba lha dang mi rnams kyi ston pa de chos ston to//thog mar dge ba/bar du dge ba/tha mar dge ba/don 
10a2(rgyud): bzang po//tshig 'bru bzang po//ma 'dres pa/yongasu rdzog pa/yongs su dag pa/yongs su dkar ba/tshangs par spyod pa yang dag par ston to//
10a3(rgyud): de da ltar bzhugs te 'tsho zhing bzhes la chos ston to//sngags kyi spyod pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod dang /lam gsum gyi spyod pa'i lam sgrub par byed

10b1(rgyud): dang /byang sgrol dang /mir skye ba nyid thob pa nyid kyi chos ston par byed de//gang du yang sems can gyi ris skal pa med pa dang /srid pa gsum yang dag par 
10b2(rgyud): 'joms par byed pa'i lam dang /gtan du grub pa dang /bde ba 'thun pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa la sems can rnams bkod par byed pa'i bcom ldan 'das 
10b3(rgyud): shAkya thub pa'i brtson 'grus ni ngo mtshar to//bcom ldan 'das bsams gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ni sangs rgyas bcom 
10b4(rgyud): ldan 'das kyi mkhyen par gyur gyis/bdag gi ci zhig 'tshal ba rngo thog/dbang phyug gi rnam par 'phrul pa bsam gyis mi khyab/rmad du byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
10b5(rgyud): 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas rnams kyi rnam par 'phrul pa shes par 'am/'tshal bar ram/thugs kyi spyad pa spyod pa'i rjesu 'jug pa'i sgrub pa dang /spyod pa 'tshal 
10b6(rgyud): bar ram/mdor na bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong gi bar du yang bstan par mi nus so//gang yang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
10b7(rgyud): mdzad pa bsdus pa 'am/tha dad pa ma lus pa'i ngo bo 'di ni chos 'dus ma byas su lta bar byas te/mthong bas spang bar bya ba la rten pa 'am/
11a1(rgyud): @#/ O /yon tan ni bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa khe nas mkhyen pa yin gyi/bdag gis ni ma lags so//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas 
11a2(rgyud): rang gi rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul pas rin po che'i pad ma'i sprul pa la com ldan 'das shAkya thub pa la blta zhing 'dug go/de nas bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub bas byang chub sems
11a3(rgyud): dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'jam dpal gzhon nur rgyur pa/de bzhin gshegs pa dang rjes su 'thun pa'i gtam rnam pa sna tshogs sngon du 'gro ba mdzad nas chos stan pa dang rjes 
11a4(rgyud): su 'thun pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa sgrub pa dang rjes su 'thun pas/tshangs pa'i dbyangs dang /ka la ping ka'i skad dang /'brug sgra dang /rnga bo che'i sgra skad kyi 
11a5(rgyud): gsung gi dbyangs kyis 'jam dpal gzhon nurgyur pa la bka' stsal pa/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa khyod legs par 'ongs sam/sems dpa' chen po'i spyod pa 
11a6(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byin gyis brlabs pa sgrub pa/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi don thob par byed pa/sngags thams cad kyi tshig dang /gsang ba dang /bcas 
11a7(rgyud): pa dang /dbang bskur ba dang /phyag rgya dang /dkyil 'khor dang /cho ga dang /tshe dang /nad med pa dang /dbang phyug dang /bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed

30a3(rgyud): dpal ldan ma dang /dpa' me dang /dpa' bzang me dang /shin tu mi bzad ma dang /mi bzang ldan ma dang /lha mdzes ma dang /ro langs ma dang /gsang ba dam pa ma dang /shing ma la 
30a4(rgyud): gnas ma dang /mya ngan med ma dang /mun par mdzes ma dang /'od ldan ma dang /lhag par ldan ma dang /gzugs mdzes ma dang /dkar med ma dang /zhi ba ma dang /phyegs

30a7(rgyud): mo rgya stong phrag du ma'i 'khor dang ldan pa 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor der rgyud ring po nyid nas/bcom ldan 'das shAkya grub pa la phyag 'tshal zhing me cod par gyur te//'di lta ste/sdud ma dang /
30a8(rgyud): phyag dam khred kyi sha za me dang /sha za me drag me dang /sha za me skar mda' dang /sha za me 'bar ma dang /thal ba za ma dang /sha za dang /dug ma dang /bung ba dang /rmengs byed ma dang /

30b2(rgyud): gdul dka' ma dang /a le ma dang /me chen byed ma de/du la gnas ma dang /du ba mo ste/sha za me chen me 'di dag kyang sha za mo'i 'khor bye ba brgya stong phrag du ma dang bcas pa di dag 
30b3(rgyud): kyang 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor chen po der 'dus shing rab tu zhugs par gyur te//gang yang ma med dang /ma me chen me dag 'jig rten gyi rjes su rnam par spyod cing sems can rnams la 'tsho bar 
30b4(rgyud): byed de/gter ma dang /me tog dang sman la spyed par byed pa dag la/'di lta ste/'ongs pa ma dang /dbang phyug chen po ma dang /khyab 'jug ma dang /gzhon nu ma dang /rgan byed ma dang /
30b5(rgyud): phag mo dang /dbang me dang /gshin rje me dang /me phyogs ma dang /nyi ma'i bu me dang /'jig rten mthar byed ma dang /chu lha ma dang /dbang ldan ma dang /rlung phyogs ma dang /
30b6(rgyud): gzhan gyi srog 'phrog ma dang /mgo bregs ma dang /bya ma dang /bya ma chen me dang /srul me dang /lus srul mo dang /skrab byed ma ste/ma me chen mo de dag ma mo'i 'khor bye ba 
30b7(rgyud): khri khrag rgya stong du ma dang /bcas pa de dag kyang 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor chen po der sangs rgyas la phyag 'tshal lo zhes pa'i tshig 'den cing 'khod par gyur to//de bzhin du mi dang mi ma yin pa 
30b8(rgyud): bye ba khri phrag brgya stong du ma dang /sems can ma yin pa nas dmyal ba chen po mnar med pa nas/srid pa'i rtse mo'i bar du thams cad kyang skabs 'dir/nam mkha'i dkyil du 
31a1(rgyud): @#//snang bar gyur te/sems can gyi rigs na sreg chags su la yang mi 'thun pa med par gyur te//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi

31a3(rgyud): kyi spyi gtsug na bzhugs pa mthong bar gyur te//de nas bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pas thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams su sangs rgyas kyi spen gyi rnam par

31a5(rgyud): gyi le'u rab 'byams byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod gang 'di bshad pa'i dus la bab par sems pa na shig/de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyu pa/bcom ldan

31a8(rgyud): gnas lhag par gnas par gyur te//gang gnod sprin dang /srin po dang /dri za dang /yi dgas dang /rlung lha dang /sha za de dag dang /mdor    
31b1(rgyud): na sems can gyi khams thams cad byang chub sems dpa'i byin gyis brlabs kyis/gzhal med de dag rje rje nor bu rin po che lta bur gyur te/der rab tu zhugs te mkhod par 
31b2(rgyud): gyur kyang phan tshun rnam par 'tshe ba byed pa med de//de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nar gyur bas 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor chen po der 'dus par shes nas//khro bo'i rgyal 
31b3(rgyud): po gshin rje gshed la smras pa/kye khro bo chen po sangs rgyas dang /bden pa sems dpa' thams cad kyi rjes su nges par 'byung bar byed pa/'khor gyi

31b5(rgyud): sems can zhi ba dag sad par gyis shig/sems can thams cad dad par gyis shig/ji srid du nga'i rang bzhin gi sngags kyi spyod pa rjes su bsgrub pa/

31b7(rgyud): ces smras pa dang /khre bo'i rgyal pos mnyan nas/khre bo chen po'i gzugs kyi sems can thams cad rjes su bsrung ba dang /rjes su brtan pa'i phyir 'khor gyi 
31b8(rgyud): dkyil 'khor thams cad du/khro bo'i rgyal po gshin rje gshed khro ba'i 'khor brgya stong phrag du ma phyogs bzhi kun dang /steng dang /'og dang /thad ka dag tu gshin 
32a1(rgyud): @#/ O /du mi bzad pa'i sgra rab tu 'don cing 'khod par gyur to//de nas sems can de dag zhi ba dang /yid dang /sems bzang po dang ldan par gyur te/
32a2(rgyud): bka' las mi 'da' bar gyur to//'di lta bu'i sgra yang rab tu thos par gyur to//gang dam tshig 'di las 'da' bar byed pa de dag ni a rdza ka'i dog pa bzhin du mgo bo tshal 
32a3(rgyud): pa brgyar 'gas par 'gyur te/de ni byang chub sems dpa'i byin gyis brlabs kyang de yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas/
32a4(rgyud): rang gi sngags kyi spyod pa'i don du chos kyi tshig smras pa/chos gcig la yang dag par bsams na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i sngags grub pa yin te/
32a5(rgyud): gcig po gang zhe na/'di lta ste/chos thams cad spros pa med pa'i rnam par rjes su mthob ba'o//byang chub sems dpa' semsdpa' chen po chos gnyis la rab tu gnas 
32a6(rgyud): na sngags 'grub par 'gyur te/gnyis po gang zhe na/byang chub kyi sems mi gtong ba nyid dang /sems can thams cad la sems mnyam pa'o//chos gsum dang 
32a7(rgyud): ldan na sngags kyi spyod pa yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur te/gsum po gang zhe na/sems can thams cad yongs su mi gtong ba nyid dang /byang chub sems dpa'i tshul
32a8(rgyud): khrims kyi sdom pa bsrung ba dang /rang gi sngags yongs su gtong ba nyid do//chos bzhi dang yang dag par ldan na/dang po sems bskyed pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i 
32b1(rgyud): sngags 'grub par 'gyur te/bzhi po gang zhe na/rang gi sngags yongs su mi gtong ba nyid dang /gzhan gyi sngags rjes su dpyod pa mang ba nyid dang /sems can thams 
32b2(rgyud): cad la byams pas gnas pa nyid dang /snying rje chen po yongs su bsgom pa'i sems dpa' nyid dang /chos bzhi po 'di dag dang ldan na/dang po sems bskyed pa'i byang chub 
32b3(rgyud): sems dpa'i sngags grub par 'gyur ro//chos lnga dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod la 'jug cing /sngags kyi spyod pa bsgrub pa yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur te/
32b4(rgyud): lnga gang zhe na/rnam par dben pa'i phyogs yang dag par brtan pa nyid dang /bas mtha'i dgon pa brtan par byed pa nyid dang /sems can gzhan la zhe sdang med pa 
32b5(rgyud): nyid dang /'jig rten pa'i sngags la mi lta ba nyid dang /tshul khrims dang /thos pa dang /spyod pa la gnas par byed pa nyid de/chos lnga po 'di dag dang ldan na sngags spyod pa'i 
32b6(rgyud): don grub cing /yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro//chos drug dang ldan na sngags kyi spyod pa grub cing yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur/drug po gang zhe na/dkon mchog gsum po la 
32b7(rgyud): dad pa dang rjes su spyod pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' la dad pa rjes su mi spyod pa nyid dang /'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i sngags la kun tu dga' ba nyid dang /chos
32b8(rgyud): kyi dbyings spros pa med pa la blta ba nyid dang /theg pa chen po'i mdo sde zab pa'i tshig gi don mi spong zhing /skyo ba med pa'i sems nyid dang /sngags kyi spyod pa yongs su btsal ba nyid dang /
33a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dge ba'i phyogs yongs su mi gtong pa nyid de/chos drug po 'di dag dang ldan na rig pa'i spyod pa dang ldan zhing sngags grub cing yang dag par sgrub pa nyid du 'gyur ro//

33a7(rgyud): pa nyid dang /spros pa med pa'i sems can gyi khams kyi chos kyi dbyings kyi de bzhin nyid la 'jug pa gnyis su med pa'i ngo bo'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes yongs 
33a8(rgyud): su 'tshol pa nyid dang /sems can thams cad yongs su mi gtong ba nyid dang /theg pa dman pa la dga' ba med pa nyid de/chos bdun po dag gi ni rig pa dang /sngags
33b1(rgyud): grub cing yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro//chos brgyad dang ldan na/rig pa dang sngags kyi spyod pa'i don grub cing lam du 'gro bar 'gyur te/brgyad po gang zhe na/mthong 
33b2(rgyud): ba dang /mthong ba'i 'bras bu dag pa la blta ba dang /sems par 'dod pa nyid dang /byang chub sems dpa'i 'bras bu dang bcas pa'i rdzu 'phrul dang /rnam par    
33b3(rgyud): 'phrul pa nyid la the tshom med cing dang ba nyid dang /phyin ci ma log pa'i sngags bzung ba dang /bla ma la gus pa nyid dang /sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dang nyid

33b5(rgyud): pa rmi lam na mthong ba rab tu ston pa la mkhas pa nyid dang /ser sna dang /gnyid dang /rmugs pa mtha' dag dang bral bar byed cing /rtag tu brtson 'grus brtsom

33b8(rgyud): pa dang /dbang che bar grag pa'i gang zag la bsnyen pa dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen dang mi 'bral bar bya ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' 'jam dpal gzhon
